# Agents · D · part 34

995 entries, docs-writer to documentary-editor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **docs-writer**: Proposes documentation-only updates based on verified Touchline source changes. ([CarlosSardo/touchline](https://github.com/CarlosSardo/touchline/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/docs-writer.agent.md) · ★1)
- **docs-writer**: Fase DOCS do ciclo SDD e manutenção da documentação. Use ao FIM de uma feature para refletir o comportamento final na spec e nos docs, ou quando o pedido é explicitamente documental (atualizar `docs/*.md`, o mapa de módulos/invariantes do `CLAUDE.md`, README, ou a especificação viva em `docs/sdd/specification.md`). Escreve no estilo denso e preciso dos docs existentes, sob a régua de qualidade de documentação de elite (benchmark + 5 lentes). Não altera código de produção. ([celfons/ai-first](https://github.com/celfons/ai-first/blob/HEAD/agents/docs-writer.md) · ★1)
- **docs-writer**: Writes and maintains the crate's documentation (README.md, docs/**, the rustdoc's module pages) so that it explains the problem solved and the trade-off taken, not only the mechanics. Use when the API, an error, a default, a guarantee or a verification result changes, when a decision needs a record, and when a page must be brought back in line with the code. ([chussenot/judgment](https://github.com/chussenot/judgment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/docs-writer.md) · ★1)
- **docs-writer**: Use this agent to draft or update a single page under docs/docs/** in the Hyveon Docusaurus site. The agent verifies every claim against the real code before writing and returns a change summary plus the sources it checked, not the prose. ([CoderCoco-Studios/Hyveon](https://github.com/CoderCoco-Studios/Hyveon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/docs-writer.md) · ★1)
